## Corpus Check
- 5199 files · ~6,078,123 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 31 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .csv 4, .css 4)

## Summary
- 57780 nodes · 183647 edges · 1450 communities (1183 shown, 267 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 22262 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0751ee93`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
